class op12_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 7, p1: 8}
    index0 = p1
    index1 = -p0 + p1
    index2 = p0
    index3 = 8*p0 + p1
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(7, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr, constant)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(lt, 0.0)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index3')
        store = ops.store('buf12', get_index_1, masked_subblock1, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(1, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(ge, constant_1, constant_2)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_2 = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.int64)
        gt = ops.gt(index_expr_1, index_expr_2)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(gt, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        mul = ops.mul(where, to_dtype)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_3', get_index_3)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(load, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        add = ops.add(mul, to_dtype_1)
        constant_3 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        eq = ops.eq(add, constant_3)
        get_index_4 = self.get_index('index1')
        index_expr_3 = ops.index_expr(get_index_4, torch.int64)
        constant_4 = ops.constant(1, torch.int64)
        ge_1 = ops.ge(index_expr_3, constant_4)
        constant_5 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        constant_6 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where_1 = ops.where(ge_1, constant_5, constant_6)
        get_index_5 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_4 = ops.index_expr(get_index_5, torch.int64)
        get_index_6 = self.get_index('index2')
        index_expr_5 = ops.index_expr(get_index_6, torch.int64)
        gt_1 = ops.gt(index_expr_4, index_expr_5)
        to_dtype_2 = ops.to_dtype(gt_1, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(where_1, to_dtype_2)
        constant_7 = ops.constant(-3.4028234663852886e+38, torch.float32)
        where_2 = ops.where(eq, constant_7, mul_1)
        get_index_7 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_7)
        logical_not = ops.logical_not(load_1)
        logical_not_1 = ops.logical_not(logical_not)
        to_dtype_3 = ops.to_dtype(logical_not_1, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bool)
        mul_2 = ops.mul(where_2, to_dtype_3)
        return mul_2
